- RCOV.P1 — Receipt-coverage hunt engine: Friday cron now pulls→sweeps(rejected finds→needs_attention)→seeds; */5min `process-receipt-hunts` cron drains the hunt queue (IMAP search of operator inboxes via Gmail app passwords + X-GM-RAW, Claude scoring w/ 0.8-confidence + 2c/2% amount corroboration, $15/wk LLM budget guard) and enqueues finds into invoices_queue (`email_hunt` source, content-hash deduped, mig 370) then finalizes the weekly report v2 (found/in-review/needs-attention/chase-list) + weekly heartbeat on clean cycles only. Hunt-inboxes card on /accounting (live IMAP verify; app passwords per un1t-finance-agent operator doc). OPERATOR: add the 3 inboxes on /accounting; mig 370 must be applied before merge.
- RCOV.P2 — Audit findings closed + /accounting matured: F2 VAT cross-check at Xero push (create-response TotalTax vs OCR tax_amount, >2c → `xero_tax_mismatch`, mig 371 + inbox badge); Exceptions tab (VAT mismatches, aging DRAFT bills F3, bills-missing-attachment F5, receiptless-expected F4, stuck-rows) + Runs & health tab (heartbeat staleness, mailbox health, run history, 7d LLM spend); per-line row actions ignore/unignore/rehunt/upload (upload = 25MB sniffed multipart → content-hash-deduped supplier_email queue row → line submitted; ignore/upload clear hunt flags — finalizer-wedge guard); new Accounting sidebar group (/accounting + /invoices + /card-receipts moved out of Work). Mig 371 applied via MCP.
- RCOV hotfix — Xero reconnect broke ("Sorry, something went wrong"): 'accounting.reports.read' is a RETIRED broad scope under Xero's 2026 granular migration and gets rejected at the AUTHORIZE step. Scope swapped to granular 'accounting.banktransactions.read'; coverage pull re-sourced from /Reports/BankStatement to paginated /BankTransactions (client-side IsReconciled filter preserves the zero-rows tripwire; stable bt:<id> line keys). scopes.test.js now pins the retired strings. Known limitation: unactioned statement lines are invisible to this source — Finance API BankStatementsPlus is the candidate upgrade (verify scope before requesting).
- CI fix — check:route-guards flagged the four RCOV.P2 per-line coverage routes: they delegate auth to the shared loadLineForUser helper (full guard: session → accounting_hub → location-scoped 404-not-403 lookup, src/app/api/accounting/coverage/[id]/_line.js) which was never added to the script's SESSION_GUARDS allowlist. Allowlisted with verification comment. Routes were secure throughout — checker gap, not an auth gap. (Process note: gate commands piped through `tail` mask failing exit codes — the local CI mirror must run checks bare or with pipefail.)
- RCOV — coverage tracks MONEY OUT only (Richard's call): inbound money (member payments, merchant settlements) excluded from tracked lines — 75 of the first probe's 83 rows were Revolut Merchant settlements drowning the board. Inbound rows still counted by the zero-rows tripwire. One-time cleanup deleted already-inserted inbound rows. (Probe also confirmed all 9 bank accounts pull correctly.)
- RCOV — coverage pull re-sourced to Finance API BankStatementsPlus (statement-lines.js, scope finance.bankstatementsplus.read): /BankTransactions can't see imported statement lines nobody has actioned — Richard hit it same day (Revolut EUR Main: 2 lines on the board vs more in Xero's reconcile screen). Endpoint/scope/fields verified against Xero's own OpenAPI spec (xero-finance.yaml) BEFORE requesting the scope (the retired-scope lesson, now applied). One call per account (no pagination on this endpoint, SummaryOnly=false required); stable sl:<statementLineId> keys; isReconciled filtered client-side to preserve the zero-rows tripwire; money-out-only carried over. bank-transactions.js kept inactive as fallback until the new source is probe-proven. Operators must RECONNECT Xero once more for the new scope. One-time cleanup deleted the bt:* rows (fresh start on sl:* keys — avoids a spurious mass-cover trip).
- RCOV — REVERTED the BankStatementsPlus source same day: Richard's reconnect failed AUTHORIZE with "invalid_scope / Error code: 500". Root cause: the Finance API is ENTITLEMENT-GATED per app (built for lending use-cases) — the scope string existing in xero-finance.yaml does NOT mean a standard app may request it. Lesson upgraded: verify entitlement, not just spec-existence; scopes.test.js now pins ALL finance.* scopes as forbidden alongside the retired broad scopes. pull.js restored to the /BankTransactions source (worked live: all 9 accounts); statement-lines.js kept on disk marked INACTIVE (if Xero ever grants Finance API access, PR #802 is the swap). Accepted limitation stands: unactioned imported statement lines are invisible — those items surface once the bookkeeper actions them in Xero, and the /accounting upload/hunt flow covers everything the API can see.
- RCOV — statement CSV bridge (Richard's pick after the Finance API dead-end): operator exports statement lines from Xero's UI per bank account and uploads on the Coverage tab — the ONLY way unactioned feed lines (the real reconcile backlog) reach the board. New statement-csv.js (tolerant header-mapped parser: signed Amount OR Spent/Received pair, Irish date formats, loud failure listing found headers; identity reuses P0 assignOrdinals/computeLineKey over the FULL parse) + import-statement.js (INSERT-ONLY + explicit cover — a csv: line is covered only when a later upload shows the same key Reconciled; NO vanish-cover since exports are arbitrary windows; cross-source (date,amount) dedupe skips lines already tracked as bt:). LOAD-BEARING: syncBankLines' vanish-cover select now excludes the csv: namespace — without that, every API refresh would mass-cover all CSV-imported lines (pinned in coverage.test.js). Routes: POST /api/accounting/coverage/import-statement (422 on unrecognised shape), GET /api/accounting/coverage/accounts (picker). Finance API access request submitted with Xero (Financial Web form) — if granted, PR #802 is the automated replacement and the CSV bridge becomes the fallback.
- TV-TEMPLATE.6–8 — TV template usability round (operator-reported: pasted text always too large / templates "don't work"): (1) zone fontSize is now a MAXIMUM, not an exact size — useFitText in the shared TemplateCanvas shrinks text (capped iterative scrollHeight/Width measure, 9px legibility floor clamped to never exceed the max, re-fit on web-font load) so pasted paragraphs fit instead of clipping; template editor preview + TV + push preview all agree. (2) Mobile TvTemplateCanvas rewritten to the web math (contain-frame vs the old cover/box-relative render that lied for non-16:9 images; resolveZone + colorRuns via shared/tv-template — src/lib/tv-template.js is now a re-export shim; RN auto-fit via adjustsFontSizeToFit + height-derived numberOfLines) and TvPushModal got a live read-only preview — mobile pushes are no longer blind. (3) TV-REMEMBER.1: reopening the push modal for a TV showing a template pre-selects it and seeds zones from the live template_values (legacy string values normalised; switching templates starts from that template's defaults) — recurring boards keep their styling, both platforms. (4) TV-REMEMBER.2: "Now showing" thumbnails on both TV lists (img for storage/url, read-only canvas render for template pushes; deleted template → text row only). (5) TV-TEMPLATE.7: Montserrat (next/font, NEXA stand-in) on zone text + the idle clock. No migration — fontSize semantics change is render-time only.
- RCOV — statement CSV bridge now reads Xero's Bank Reconciliation report export (operator-reported: import failed "Couldn't find the statement columns"). That report has no column headers — it's a sectioned layout — so the header-mapped parser hit the report title. parseStatementCsv now tries header-mode first, then falls back to reconciliation-report mode: it reads ONLY the "Plus Unreconciled Statement Lines" section (bank-feed lines Xero hasn't reconciled = the receipt backlog; other sections are Xero-side and already API-visible), positional cols Date/Payee/Description/Amount, parens = money out. Verified against Richard's real export: 109 lines → 59 money-out tracked, 50 income excluded, 0 warnings. UI hint updated to point at Reports → Bank Reconciliation → Export. Plain statement CSV export still works. (LESSON: fresh-branch-off-origin/main — a stale local main showed 4 phantom finalize failures fixed 25 commits upstream.)
- RCOV — "Clear board" button (operator recovery hatch for a mistaken statement import): deletes every OPEN (non-terminal) bank line for the active location — uncovered/submitted/not_found/needs_attention — plus their recon_hunts children (FK has no cascade, so children go first). Covered/ignored history and receipts already pushed to Xero are untouched; recoverable via Refresh from Xero (re-pulls bt:) + re-upload (re-adds csv:). window.confirm states the count + recoverability. Audited as a recon_runs row (trigger 'clear', stats {cleared, clearedBy}) — visible in Runs & health. Mig 373 widens recon_runs_trigger_check to allow 'clear' (applied to prod). Lib clearOpenLines is paginated + 300-chunked + best-effort audit; route POST /api/accounting/coverage/clear (session → accounting_hub → active location).
- RCOV — hunt feedback on the Coverage board (operator: "click Re-hunt, nothing happens"). The hunt was working — it runs on the */5min drain cron, not in-request, and the board showed no sign a search was queued. Now: the board GET returns hunt_queued_at/hunt_claimed_at/last_hunted_at/hunt_attempts; a queued line shows a "Searching inbox…" chip and its Re-hunt button becomes a disabled "Searching…"; a banner explains the background search runs within ~5 min; the board auto-polls every 60s while any line is queued so the outcome (In review / Not found) appears without a manual reload; not_found lines show "searched N× — no receipt found" so a genuine miss reads as searched, not skipped. (Verified live: first real hunts ran — Lucy Stapleton €406.25 FOUND + submitted, Three Ireland €163.15 not_found — proving IMAP + scoring end-to-end against stillorgan@un1t.com.)
- RCOV — hunt inboxes are now PER LOCATION (operator: on SourceIt's accounting page saw Stillorgan's 3 inboxes — breaks per-location independence, and worse, a cross-entity data leak: a SourceIt bank line could be searched against a Stillorgan inbox). recon_mailboxes was built global (no location_id). Mig 374 adds location_id (existing 3 rows backfilled to Stillorgan, where they were created), swaps the global unique(email) for unique(location_id,email), indexes location_id. hunt.js activeMailboxes(db, locationId) now filters to the LINE's location (load-bearing isolation, via .match to keep the single-terminal test mock). Routes (GET/POST list+add, [id] DELETE, health mailbox panel) all scope to the active location — add sets location_id, delete is location-scoped (404 on another location's id), list shows only this location's. HuntInboxesCard copy updated. Same inbox can be added to >1 location independently (two rows).
- Platform — location switch now updates the main view, not just the sidebar (operator: switching location changed the sidebar but the page stayed on the old location until a manual refresh). Root cause: LocationSwitcher sets the cookie + calls router.refresh(), which re-runs server components and refreshes the Sidebar's `user` prop, but client components inside a page fetch their data once on mount and don't re-run on a soft refresh. Fix: AppShell keys the page subtree (`<Fragment key={user.activeLocation.id}>`) so it remounts on a location switch — client components re-fetch for the new location. Key changes only on a location switch (not normal navigation); Fragment adds no DOM, zero layout impact. Estate-wide (every page).
- RCOV — Aged Payables tab on /accounting (Richard: "see who we owe money to and how overdue it is"). New Payables tab pulls unpaid AUTHORISED ACCPAY bills live from the active location's Xero connection (GET /Invoices, summaryOnly, paginated), aggregates per supplier with a standard aging ladder (not due / 1-30 / 31-60 / 61-90 / 90+), sorted most-overdue-first, with total-owed / overdue / 90d+ / supplier-count summary cards and a per-supplier oldest-overdue chip. Chose the invoices endpoint over AgedPayablesByContact (which is strictly per-contact — contactID mandatory — so a whole-supplier view would be N calls). Scope accounting.invoices (already held) — NO new Xero authorisation. Per-location (uses that location's connection); live read, no cached table. Pure lib src/lib/xero/aged-payables.js (mapPayableInvoices + daysOverdue + bucketFor + agePayables) unit-tested; route GET /api/accounting/payables (409 if Xero not connected). Read-only for now — no pay/action buttons.
- Xero account picker showed too few accounts (operator: SourceIt bill picker showed 4 accounts, not its chart). The picker filtered to Xero AccountType 'EXPENSE' only, but standard Irish/UK charts (SourceIt = Givers Consultancy) class day-to-day expenses as OVERHEADS (18: General Expenses, Travelling, Legal/Accountancy Fees, Bank Charges, Subsistence, Computers & Software…) and DIRECTCOSTS (7: Cost of Sales) — only 4 accounts were literally type EXPENSE, so the picker hid the 25 accounts operators actually code bills to. Fix: GET /api/locations/[id]/xero/accounts now defaults to type=SPEND = the full bill-codeable set (EXPENSE, OVERHEADS, DIRECTCOSTS, FIXED, CURRENT, CURRLIAB — the debit side; excludes income/equity/bank). XeroAccountPicker requests ?type=SPEND. type=ALL and single-AccountType still supported. Data was fully synced (66 active accounts) — the filter was the bug, which is why Refresh didn't help.
- DASH-M.1 — mobile Business dashboard rebuilt to command-centre block parity with the web /dashboard/business (DASH-REBUILD): briefing line, 4 headline KPIs (Revenue MTD / Members / Churn risk / In arrears), "Needs you" rail, acquisition funnel + ads-7d, membership live + 12-month stacked-bar trend (plain Views, no chart dep), today-ops strip. Data flows through ONE new authenticated JSON route GET /api/dashboard/business (neutral prefix, NOT /api/mobile/* — standing decision; registered in openapi.js) with per-block failure isolation (failed block = null key → compact error cell). The page's embedded KPI/briefing composition was extracted VERBATIM to src/lib/dashboard/business-kpis.js (buildBusinessKpis) so web + route consume the same computation — no duplicated KPI logic; unit-tested (degradation rules: revenue failure kills the block, everything else degrades quietly). Old mobile pipeline/labour cards dropped (pipeline lives in the Pipeline tab; labour is in the today strip) — fetchBusinessDashboardData removed from shared/dashboard-data.js (its only consumer). Segment gating unchanged (top-level dashboard_business key, checked again server-side in the route).
- WATECH.AV — public /technical service page for Meta Tech Provider Access Verification. Meta's form requires a live website URL showing the described B2B service + the business providing it; un1tdublin.com/technical was cited on the draft form but the route didn't exist (logged-out visitors bounced to the /welcome chooser). New standalone static page (src/app/technical/page.js) in the marketing design language (in-page Poppins per the /event/[slug] pattern, own #lp-shell so lp-reveal arms outside the /welcome layout, SiteHeader wordmark fallback + SiteFooter): hero, 4 numbered platform features (shared WA+IG inbox, gym CRM, AI assistant with human takeover, in-chat booking), a Platform-Data handling section (official Meta APIs, per-gym isolation, opt-in/opt-out, links to /privacy + /privacy/authority-requests + /terms), and provider details (UN1T Dublin Ltd + contact). Allowlisted in all THREE public-path gates (proxy.js publicPaths, brands.js un1t-marketing allowedPaths, AppShell PUBLIC_PATHS — the #923 lesson). Copy deliberately mirrors the Access Verification form description (reviewers cross-check site vs form). No DB, no API, no migration.
- RCOV — hunt-queue wedge guards + finalizer 48h bound (found during the 2026-07-18 stale-heartbeat diagnosis; the LIVE stale cause is CCF Autos' pre-coverage-scopes Xero grant, fixed by reconnect, not code). Three latent bugs closed: (1) maybeFinalizeWeekly's pending-hunts gate now mirrors claim_recon_hunt_batch's status filter (uncovered/not_found + hunt_queued_at) — a queued row in any other status is unclaimable by BOTH drain paths (cron RPC + QStash worker CAS), so counting it wedged the finalizer at hunts_pending forever, silently killing the weekly report AND heartbeat; (2) the re-hunt route re-opens a needs_attention line as 'uncovered' in the same queue update (it allowed needs_attention but the claim paths never claim that status — one Re-hunt click on a rejected line = a permanently-queued unclaimable row; rejected-find exclusion + invoices_queue_id audit link unchanged); (3) syncBankLines' vanish→covered flip clears hunt_queued_at/hunt_claimed_at (covered is terminal; a line reconciled in Xero while queued stayed marked queued forever). Plus locationCronRun now enforces its documented 48h bound (gte started_at now−RECENT_CRON_MS) — without it a location whose pull stopped running kept satisfying allClean on a stale old run. Pure-lib + route-helper tests (rehuntPatch extracted per the QStash-worker export pattern). No migration — claim_recon_hunt_batch untouched.
- OPENAPI — registered POST /api/dashboard/ads/refresh in src/lib/openapi.js (PR #916): the pre-existing /dashboard/ads "Refresh" route predated PR #912's Dashboard section and was missing from /api/openapi.json and /api-docs. Dashboard tag, CookieAuth, body { locationId }, passthrough 200, ErrorResponse 400/401/403 (dashboard_ads permission). Spec-only — no route code changes.
- MOBILE-SDK57 — Expo SDK 54→57 (RN 0.81.5→0.86.0, React 19.1→19.2.3, reanimated 4.5.0 + worklets 0.10.0; babel plugin → react-native-worklets/plugin; expo-splash-screen/status-bar/web-browser registered as config plugins; runtimeVersion → 2.0.0 = new OTA lane — align with the store release; the 5 useHeaderHeight imports → 'expo-router/react-navigation'). SEAM CHANGE: Metro 0.84's on-demand file map is scoped to the project root, so the relative '../shared' escapes died ("Unable to resolve" / "Failed to get the SHA-1") — ../shared is now consumed as a real package ("shared": "file:../shared" → node_modules symlink), 33 imports across 29 files rewritten to 'shared/<module>'; vitest.config.js gained a matching `shared` alias so root npm test resolves mobile/lib tests without mobile/node_modules (web-ci installs root deps only). check:mobile-imports guard REBUILT for the new specifier — the symlinked resolution landed inside node_modules and import/ignore silently disabled the guard (probe-proven: bogus 'shared/permissions' name exited 0); resolver now sets preserveSymlinks:false + an absolute repo-root paths fallback, probe-verified to catch the verbatim 2026-06 MANAGER_ROLES crash import with AND without mobile/node_modules present. Validated: expo export iOS (2257 modules), expo-doctor 20/20, npm ci --dry-run, full CI mirror. NOT an OTA — whole-SDK native change; ship as a store release (existing 1.4.x installs freeze until updated). Verify once on expo.dev: GitHub App "Base directory" = mobile.
